当 疫苗接种 遇到感染预防:麻疹 国家住院患者管理实践 - 腮腺炎 - 风疹、水痘和轮状病毒 疫苗。

When Vaccination Meets Infection Prevention: National Practices for Inpatient Administration of Measles-Mumps-Rubella, Varicella, and Rotavirus Vaccines.
作者： Maria Susana Rueda Altez, Celia Hutto, Delphene Hobby-Noland, Xiaoyan Song
摘要中文翻译在接受调查的儿科机构中,尽管感染预防措施各不相同,尤其是 VAR,但 MMR、VAR 和 RV 的管理经常被允许。大多数机构都采取了标准预防措施,并且报告没有 疫苗 衍生的 传播。尽管存在持续的安全问题和有限的正式政策,但大多数受访者认为收益大于风险。

When Vaccination Meets Infection Prevention: National Practices for Inpatient Administration of Measles-Mumps-Rubella, Varicella, and Rotavirus Vaccines.

Authors: Maria Susana Rueda Altez, Celia Hutto, Delphene Hobby-Noland, Xiaoyan Song
Original abstract (English)
Among pediatric institutions surveyed, administration of MMR, VAR, and RV was frequently allowed, though infection-prevention practices varied, particularly for VAR. Most institutions used standard precautions and reported no vaccine-derived transmission. Despite ongoing safety concerns and limited formal policies, most respondents believed benefits outweighed risks.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

暴发 响应中的沟通和能力:疫苗接种 在以色列 2025 年至 2026 年期间对极端正统社区的外展 麻疹 暴发:一项混合方法研究

Communication and capacity in outbreak response: vaccination outreach to ultra-Orthodox communities during Israel's 2025 to 2026 measles outbreak: a mixed-methods study
作者： Eran Kopel, Ravit Bassal
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 以色列自 2018-19 年以来最大的 麻疹 传播 可追溯到 2025 年 4 月的一次输入,传播 在极端正统犹太社区持续存在;截至 2026 年 4 月,已记录 3,624 例病例和 17 例死亡。有两种解释相互竞争:社区态度和消息传递,或者 疫苗接种 的实际可用性;这项研究对两者进行了检验。方法 混合方法回顾性研究,2024年1月至2026年8月,以截至2025年3月的月份为基线。合并了六个数据流:流行病学指标;卫生部关于 麻疹 的人口普查新闻稿;169 个极端正统媒体项目,通过针对框架、引用权威和主题的定向内容分析进行编码,并根据 20% 的样本评估编码器间的可靠性;疫苗接种 访问中一系列已记录的日期更改;政府采购登记册;和反疫苗接种材料。通过卡方检验和费舍尔精确检验比较不同时期的分布。结果 在 暴发 之前,两个渠道都很安静:2024 年有 10 个部委发布和 10 个社区项目。社区媒体中支持 疫苗接种 的宣传从 暴发 之前项目的 8.3% 上升到升级期间的 30.9%,并在 2026 年下降到 17.6%(p = 0.10)。2025 年 5 月至 12 月期间,有 14 个项目引用了拉比的声音(Fisher p = 0.041),其中 5 个项目是医疗援助组织的主席,而不是伊斯兰教权威。没有任何项目放大拒绝。在采取包括上门接种和延长 疫苗接种 服务时间在内的措施后,两个月内,1 至 6 岁儿童的第一剂接种覆盖率从耶路撒冷的 77% 提高到 84%,贝特谢梅什从 72% 提高到 82.6%。通信和 疫苗 访问并不总是一起进行:官方通信的高峰月没有带来有记录的访问扩展,并且公开的 疫苗 电台列表从 2025 年 8 月到 2026 年 1 月没有变化。超正统领域的合同外展阵列于 2020 年招标,于 2023 年 10 月结束,没有续签;从那时起,暴发 的外展活动就依赖于小型的、免招标的活动。结论 极端正统媒体是一个接受渠道,拉比和社区参与一旦激活就会发挥作用。主要的限制是能力,而不是说服力:固定的外展基础设施、物理访问和维护的公共信息。在 麻疹 之间维持这些,并将通信与其促进的访问保持一致,是 麻疹 控制的优先事项。

摘要背景 以色列自 2018-19 年以来最大的 麻疹 传播 可追溯到 2025 年 4 月的一次输入,传播 在极端正统犹太社区持续存在;截至 2026 年 4 月,已记录 3,624 例病例和 17 例死亡。
核心方法
方法 混合方法回顾性研究,2024年1月至2026年8月,以截至2025年3月的月份为基线。
主要结果
结果 在 暴发:2024 年发布 10 个部委版本和 10 个社区项目之前,两个渠道都很安静。
解释与创新

科研与公卫意义
在 麻疹 之间维持这些,并将通信与其促进的访问保持一致,是 麻疹 控制的优先事项。
局限与证据强度
主要的限制是能力,而不是说服力:固定的外展基础设施、物理访问和维护的公共信息。

Communication and capacity in outbreak response: vaccination outreach to ultra-Orthodox communities during Israel's 2025 to 2026 measles outbreak: a mixed-methods study

Authors: Eran Kopel, Ravit Bassal
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Israel's largest measles outbreak since 2018-19 was traced to a single importation in April 2025, with transmission sustained in ultra-Orthodox Jewish communities; by April 2026, 3,624 cases and 17 deaths were recorded. Two explanations compete: community attitudes and messaging, or the physical availability of vaccination; this study examines both. Methods Mixed-methods retrospective study, January 2024 to August 2026, with the months to March 2025 as baseline. Six data streams were combined: epidemiological indicators; a census of Ministry of Health press releases on measles; 169 ultra-Orthodox media items coded by directed content analysis for frame, cited authority and topic, with inter-coder reliability assessed on a 20% sample; a dated series of documented changes in vaccination access; the government procurement register; and counter-vaccination material. Distributions were compared across periods by chi-square and Fisher exact tests. Results Both channels were quiet before the outbreak: 10 ministry releases and 10 community items in 2024. Pro-vaccination advocacy in community media rose from 8.3% of items pre-outbreak to 30.9% during escalation and fell to 17.6% in 2026 (p = 0.10). A rabbinic voice was cited in 14 items, all between May and December 2025 (Fisher p = 0.041), five of them chairs of medical-aid organisations rather than halachic authorities. No item amplified refusal. Access measures, including walk-in and extended-hours vaccination, were followed within two months by first-dose coverage gains among children aged 1 to 6 from 77% to 84% in Jerusalem and 72% to 82.6% in Beit Shemesh. Communication and vaccine access did not always move together: the peak month of official communication brought no documented access expansion, and the public vaccine station list was unchanged from August 2025 to January 2026. A contracted outreach array for the Ultra-Orthodox sector, tendered in 2020, ended in October 2023 and was not renewed; since then, outbreak outreach relied on small, tender-exempt engagements. Conclusions Ultra-Orthodox media were a receptive channel, and rabbinic and community engagement worked once activated. The principal constraint was capacity, not persuasion: standing outreach infrastructure, physical access and maintained public information. Sustaining these between outbreaks, and aligning communication with the access it promotes, are the priorities for measles control.

麻疹 的 流行病学 及其在埃塞俄比亚奥罗米亚地区的 监测 表现:实现 麻疹 消除目标的进展和挑战

Epidemiology of measles and its surveillance performance in the Oromia regional state of Ethiopia: Progress and challenges toward measles elimination goals
作者： Abyot Bekele Woyessa, Abebe Bekele Hurissa, Ciara E. Sugerman, Miia Vesterinen, Almea M. Matanock, Bekana Tolera, Paulos Samuel, Tesfaye Deti Galmessa, Getinet Argaw, Dabesa Gobena
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 尽管 疫苗 非常有效,并且在消除 麻疹 方面拥有数十年的经验,但 麻疹 仍然是 15 岁以下儿童的一个重大公共卫生问题,特别是在低收入和中等收入国家。功能良好的 监测 系统对于监控 麻疹 传播 和指导控制工作至关重要。为此我们分析了麻疹的流行病学及其监测系统在埃塞俄比亚奥罗米亚地区的性能。方法 通过基于病例的 麻疹 报告,分析 2006 年至 2024 年报告的疑似 麻疹 病例的流行病学特征和实验室结果。使用 R 统计软件计算频率、调整优势比 (aOR)、95% 置信区间 (CI) 和 p 值。使用非 麻疹 发热性皮疹疾病 (NMFRI) 和区级 监测 指标评估 监测 表现。结果 共报告有实验室结果的疑似麻疹病例25 674例,其中麻疹 IgM阳性9 755例(38.3%)。确诊病例中,5,173例(53.0%)为男性,78.2%为儿童

Epidemiology of measles and its surveillance performance in the Oromia regional state of Ethiopia: Progress and challenges toward measles elimination goals

Authors: Abyot Bekele Woyessa, Abebe Bekele Hurissa, Ciara E. Sugerman, Miia Vesterinen, Almea M. Matanock, Bekana Tolera, Paulos Samuel, Tesfaye Deti Galmessa, Getinet Argaw, Dabesa Gobena
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Despite the effective vaccines and decades of experience towards elimination, measles remains a significant public health problem among children ≤ 15 years, particularly in low- and middle-income countries. A well-functioning surveillance system is essential for monitoring measles transmission and guiding control efforts. Towards this end we analyzed the epidemiology of measles and its surveillance system performance in the Oromia region, Ethiopia. Methods We analyzed the epidemiologic characteristics of the suspected-measles cases with laboratory results reported during 2006–2024 through the case-based surveillance. Frequencies, adjusted odds ratios (aORs), 95% confidence-intervals (CIs), and p-values were calculated using R statistical software. Surveillance performance was assessed using the non-measles febrile rash illness (NMFRI) and district-level surveillance indicators. Results A total of 25,674 suspected-measles cases with laboratory results were reported, of which 9,755 (38.3%) were measles IgM positive. Among confirmed cases, 5,173 (53.0%) were male and 78.2% occurred among children aged

Limitations and evidence strength

麻疹 疫苗接种 在埃塞俄比亚奥罗米亚地区选定地区的及时性 – 2021

Timeliness of measles vaccination in selected districts of Oromia Region, Ethiopia – 2021
作者： Monica P. Shah, Abyot Bekele Woyessa, Almea M. Matanock, Binyam Moges Azmeraye, Leuel Lisanwork, Getnet Yimer, Shu-Hua Wang, Xueliang Pan, Paulos Samuel, Bekana Tolera
摘要中文翻译背景 延迟 麻疹 与含 麻疹 的 疫苗(MCV1、MCV2)的首剂/次剂量会增加 麻疹 传播 的风险。MCV2 于 2019 年被添加到埃塞俄比亚的常规免疫计划中,在 15 个月大时接种。我们描述了 麻疹 疫苗接种 的年龄以及与 MCV2 引入后及时 疫苗接种 相关的特征。方法 对 2021 年埃塞俄比亚奥罗米亚州 12-35 个月龄儿童进行的横断面家庭调查进行了二次分析。MCV 疫苗接种 状态和 疫苗接种 年龄是根据孩子的 疫苗接种 卡确定的。估计了按 疫苗接种 年龄划分的 MCV1 和 MCV2(24-35 个月儿童)的累积卡确认 疫苗接种 覆盖率。在接受 MCV 的儿童中,疫苗接种 时间分为早期(MCV1:<9 个月;MCV2:<15 个月)、及时(MCV1:9 至 <10 个月;MCV2:15 至 <16 个月)或延迟(MCV1:≥10 个月;MCV2:≥16 个月)。使用多元逻辑回归和城市/农村分层分析与及时 MCV1 疫苗接种 相关的特征。结果 总体而言,对 651 名 12-35 个月的儿童 (MCV1) 和 270 名 24-35 个月的儿童 (MCV2) 进行了分析。9 个月时的累积 MCV1 覆盖率为 59.9%,12 个月时为 76.3%,15 个月时的 MCV2 累积覆盖率为 33.0%,24 个月时为 44.8%。与农村地区相比,城市地区的 MCV 覆盖率普遍较高。接种儿童中,MCV1 早期接种、及时接种和延迟接种比例分别为 30.1%、44.5% 和 25.3%,MCV2 接种比例分别为 28.6%、41.2% 和 30.3%。与及时 MCV1 疫苗接种 相关的特征在城市和农村地区有所不同,但共同主题包括护理人员免疫意识和服务提供方面的提高。结论 虽然大多数接受 MCV1 的儿童在 12 个月大时就接种了疫苗,但许多人接受了延迟剂量。无论 疫苗接种 时间如何,MCV2 覆盖率都很低。研究结果强调了常规追赶疫苗接种政策和指导的重要性,以及在城乡环境中制定量身定制的策略以提高MCV覆盖率和及时疫苗接种的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景 延迟 麻疹 与含 麻疹 的 疫苗(MCV1、MCV2)的首剂/秒剂量会增加 麻疹 传播 的风险。
设计与对象
方法 对 2021 年埃塞俄比亚奥罗米亚州 12-35 个月龄儿童进行的横断面家庭调查进行了二次分析。
核心方法
使用多元逻辑回归和城市/农村分层分析与及时 MCV1 疫苗接种 相关的特征。
主要结果
与及时 MCV1 疫苗接种 相关的特征在城市和农村地区有所不同,但共同主题包括护理人员免疫意识和服务提供方面的提高。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了常规追赶疫苗接种政策和指导的重要性,以及在城乡环境中制定量身定制的策略以提高MCV覆盖率和及时疫苗接种的必要性。

局限与证据强度
结论 虽然大多数接受 MCV1 的儿童在 12 个月大时就接种了疫苗,但许多人接受了延迟剂量。

Timeliness of measles vaccination in selected districts of Oromia Region, Ethiopia – 2021

Authors: Monica P. Shah, Abyot Bekele Woyessa, Almea M. Matanock, Binyam Moges Azmeraye, Leuel Lisanwork, Getnet Yimer, Shu-Hua Wang, Xueliang Pan, Paulos Samuel, Bekana Tolera
Original abstract (English)
Background Delays in vaccination with the first/s dose of measles-containing vaccine (MCV1, MCV2) increases risk of measles transmission. MCV2 was added to Ethiopia's routine immunization schedule in 2019, administered at age 15 months. We describe age at measles vaccination and characteristics associated with timely vaccination following MCV2 introduction. Methods A secondary analysis from a 2021 cross-sectional household survey of children aged 12–35 months in Oromia, Ethiopia was conducted. MCV vaccination status and age at vaccination were determined from the child's vaccination card. The cumulative card-confirmed vaccination coverage of MCV1 and MCV2 (among children 24–35 months) by age at vaccination was estimated. Among children who received MCV, vaccination timing was classified as early (MCV1: <9 months; MCV2: <15 months), timely (MCV1: 9 to <10 months; MCV2: 15 to <16 months), or delayed (MCV1: ≥10 months; MCV2: ≥16 months). Characteristics associated with timely MCV1 vaccination were analyzed using multivariate logistic regression, with urban/rural stratification. Results Overall, 651 children aged 12–35 months (MCV1) and a subset of 270 children aged 24–35 months (MCV2) were analyzed. Cumulative MCV1 coverage was 59.9% by age 9 months and 76.3% by age 12 months, and MCV2 was 33.0% by age 15 months and 44.8% by age 24 months. MCV coverage was generally higher in urban compared to rural areas. Among vaccinated children, the proportion receiving early, timely, and delayed doses was 30.1%, 44.5%, and 25.3% for MCV1, and 28.6%, 41.2%, and 30.3% for MCV2, respectively. Characteristics associated with timely MCV1 vaccination differed in urban and rural areas, but common themes included increased caregiver immunization awareness and service delivery aspects. Conclusions Although most children who received MCV1 were vaccinated by 12 months, many received delayed doses. MCV2 coverage was low, irrespective of vaccination timing. Findings highlight the importance of routine catch-up vaccination policies and guidance, and the need for tailored strategies in urban and rural settings to improve MCV coverage and timely vaccination.

土耳其伊斯坦布尔成年人的 麻疹 免疫力下降和持续的风疹血清保护

Waning Measles Immunity and Sustained Rubella Seroprotection Among Adults in Istanbul, Türkiye
作者： Kutay Sarsar, Aytaç Acar, Sevim Meşe, Ali Ağaçfidan, Muammer Osman Köksal, Ismail Aytaç Acar

Waning Measles Immunity and Sustained Rubella Seroprotection Among Adults in Istanbul, Türkiye

Authors: Kutay Sarsar, Aytaç Acar, Sevim Meşe, Ali Ağaçfidan, Muammer Osman Köksal, Ismail Aytaç Acar
Original abstract (English)
Background and Objectives: Despite long-standing MMR vaccination programmes, outbreaks of vaccine-preventable diseases continue to occur worldwide, raising concerns regarding waning immunity and susceptibility gaps in adult populations. Between 2017 and 2019 alone, WHO reported over 880,000 measles cases globally—a fourfold increase over the preceding two years—with outbreaks in both low- and high-income countries despite high childhood vaccination coverage. This study evaluated the seroepidemiology of measles, mumps, and rubella among adults in Türkiye over a nine-year period. Materials and Methods: A retrospective laboratory-based study was conducted using ELISA IgM and IgG results obtained between 2014 and 2022 at a tertiary-care university hospital. Seropositivity rates were analysed by year, age group, and sex. Temporal trends were assessed using the Cochran–Armitage trend test; factors associated with IgG seropositivity were assessed using multivariable logistic regression. Results: Among 10,407 adult test records, overall IgG seropositivity was 75.8% for measles, 94.9% for rubella, and 86.3% for mumps; IgM positivity remained low throughout (0.9%, 0.4%, and 1.9%, respectively). Measles IgG declined significantly from 78.4% in 2014–2019 to 62.6% in 2022 (p < 0.001), whereas Rubella IgG remained stable (93.2–95.7%) and Mumps IgG showed no significant temporal change. Seropositivity increased markedly with age for measles (18–29 years: 70.2% vs. ≥50 years: 93.4%; p < 0.001) and mumps (p = 0.030). Multivariable analysis confirmed that Measles IgG seropositivity was substantially lower in 2022 than in the pooled 2014–2019 period (OR = 0.432, 95% CI: 0.319–0.584, p < 0.001). Rubella seroprotection among women of reproductive age was 94.7% (95% CI: 93.9–95.5%), close to but not statistically distinguishable from the 95% population immunity benchmark used in rubella elimination frameworks. Conclusions: A significant measles immunity gap exists among younger adults in Türkiye, with Measles IgG seropositivity declining from 78.4% in 2014–2019 to 62.6% in 2022, consistent with a cohort effect whereby vaccine-era cohorts exhibit lower protection than older adults with naturally acquired immunity. Rubella immunity, by contrast, remained close to the 95% elimination benchmark throughout. These findings underscore the importance of targeted serological surveillance and evidence-based booster vaccination strategies.

巴西东北部大型城市人口的 麻疹 免疫力:使用 PRNT 和 ELISA 进行的基于人群的评估。

Measles immunity in a large urban population in Northeast Brazil: a population-based assessment using PRNT and ELISA.
作者： Maria Eduarda Maia Vilar, Florisneide Rodrigues Barreto, Ramon da Costa Saavedra, Marcos Mauricio Tosta Leal, Olindo Assis Martins-Filho, Ana Carolina Campi-Azevedo, Andrea Teixeira-Carvalho, Cristiana Couto Garcia, Adriana de Souza Azevedo Soares, Sheila Maria Barbosa de Lima
摘要中文翻译目的:消除后环境中 麻疹 免疫力的减弱可能会增加易感性并促进病毒重新引入。本研究使用中和抗体测试和 IgG 血清学 评估了巴西东北部城市人口的 麻疹 免疫力。方法:2024 年,在巴西萨尔瓦多 30 个哨点地区的 2 岁以上居民中开展了一项基于人群的 血清流行率 研究。通过 ELISA 测量所有参与者的 麻疹 特异性 IgG 抗体,并通过分层子样本 (n=671) 中的空斑减少中和试验 (PRNT) 评估中和抗体。血清流行率 按年龄组估计。使用 Cohen's kappa 评估 ELISA 和 PRNT 之间的一致性,并使用调整年龄和性别的逻辑回归检查与 PRNT 阳性相关的因素。结果:参与者中,60.8%为女性,38.3%年龄≥50岁。PRNT 的总体 血清流行率 为 91.4%,ELISA 的总体 血清流行率 为 85.4%,测定之间的一致性为 86.3%(kappa = 0.34,p < 0.001)。PRNT 血清阳性率最高的人群为 10-19 岁和 50 岁以上人群,而最高的 PRNT 血清阴性人群为 20-29 岁的年轻人 (23.4%)。在调整后的分析中,5-19 岁的儿童和青少年 PRNT 阳性的几率低于 2-4 岁的儿童和青少年。没有观察到性别之间的显着差异。结论:尽管 血清流行率 总体较高,但仍发现了相关的免疫差距,特别是在年轻人中,表明该城市人群对 麻疹 的易感性增加。PRNT 和 ELISA 的结合使用凸显了在消除后城市环境中采取有针对性的 疫苗接种 策略的必要性。

Measles immunity in a large urban population in Northeast Brazil: a population-based assessment using PRNT and ELISA.

Authors: Maria Eduarda Maia Vilar, Florisneide Rodrigues Barreto, Ramon da Costa Saavedra, Marcos Mauricio Tosta Leal, Olindo Assis Martins-Filho, Ana Carolina Campi-Azevedo, Andrea Teixeira-Carvalho, Cristiana Couto Garcia, Adriana de Souza Azevedo Soares, Sheila Maria Barbosa de Lima
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: Waning measles immunity in post-elimination settings may increase susceptibility and facilitate viral reintroduction. This study assessed measles immunity in an urban population in Northeast Brazil using neutralizing antibody testing and IgG serology. METHODS: A population-based seroprevalence study was conducted in 2024 among residents aged ≥2 years in 30 sentinel areas of Salvador, Brazil. Measles-specific IgG antibodies were measured by ELISA in all participants, and neutralizing antibodies were assessed by plaque reduction neutralization test (PRNT) in a stratified subsample (n=671). Seroprevalence was estimated by age group. Agreement between ELISA and PRNT was evaluated using Cohen's kappa, and factors associated with PRNT positivity were examined using logistic regression adjusted for age and sex. RESULTS: Among participants, 60.8% were female and 38.3% were aged ≥50 years. Overall seroprevalence was 91.4% by PRNT and 85.4% by ELISA, with 86.3% agreement between assays (kappa = 0.34, p < 0.001). The highest PRNT seropositivity was observed among individuals aged 10-19 years and ≥50 years, whereas the highest PRNT seronegativity occurred among young adults aged 20-29 years (23.4%). In adjusted analyses, children and adolescents aged 5-19 years had lower odds of PRNT positivity compared with those aged 2-4 years. No significant differences were observed by sex. CONCLUSIONS: Despite high overall seroprevalence, relevant immunity gaps were identified, particularly among young adults, indicating increased susceptibility to measles in this urban population. The combined use of PRNT and ELISA highlights the need for targeted vaccination strategies in post-elimination urban settings.

2022-2026 年巴基斯坦旁遮普邦 麻疹 的五年描述性 流行病学:趋势、年龄分布和地理负担;一项回顾性 监测 研究

Five-year descriptive epidemiology of measles in Punjab, Pakistan, 2022–2026: trends, age distribution, and geographic burden; a retrospective surveillance study
作者： Muhammad Mehroz Saleem, Jamshaid Ahmed, Fawad Khursid, Mehwish Bhatti, Mufakker Mian

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
COVID-19 大流行后 麻疹 卷土重来,使南亚地区数百万未接种疫苗的儿童面临风险。

Five-year descriptive epidemiology of measles in Punjab, Pakistan, 2022–2026: trends, age distribution, and geographic burden; a retrospective surveillance study

Authors: Muhammad Mehroz Saleem, Jamshaid Ahmed, Fawad Khursid, Mehwish Bhatti, Mufakker Mian
Original abstract (English)
Measles resurgences following the COVID-19 pandemic have placed millions of unvaccinated children at risk across South Asia. Punjab, Pakistan’s most populous province with approximately 125 million residents, experienced a sustained measles epidemic from 2022 to 2024. This study describes the five-year epidemiology of measles in Punjab using individual-level surveillance data from the provincial Expanded Program on Immunization (EPI) surveillance system. We conducted a retrospective descriptive analysis of 72,565 suspected measles case records from the Punjab EPI VPD surveillance line-list, epidemiological week 1 of 2022 through epidemiological week 23 of 2026. Variables analyzed included case counts, incidence rates per 100,000 population, age at illness onset, sex, vaccination status, case outcomes, and district-level geographic distribution. District-level population denominators adjusted for annual growth were applied to calculate incidence rates. Statistical analysis used the Cochran-Armitage trend test, Wilson 95% confidence intervals, and Spearman rank correlation. Reported measles cases escalated from 6,613 in 2022 to a peak of 23,623 in 2024 (incidence 18.92 per 100,000 population), before declining to 19,913 in 2025 and 9,898 through week 23 of 2026. Overall, 184 deaths were recorded (case fatality ratio [CFR] 0.25%). Male cases predominated (57–58% annually; M:F ratio 1.40:1). The proportion of infants under 12 months rose from 37.3% (95% CI 36.2–38.5%) in 2022 to 47.9% (95% CI 46.9–48.9%) in 2026 (Cochran-Armitage z = 12.77, p < 0.0001), with median age at infection declining from 18 months (IQR 8–42) to 12 months (IQR 7–36). Zero-dose cases peaked at 49.6% (95% CI 49.0–50.2%) in 2024 (z = 3.37, p = 0.0008). Chiniot (62.95 per 100,000) and Jhang (59.98 per 100,000) bore the highest district incidence in 2024. Reported first-dose measles-containing vaccine (MCV1) coverage was paradoxically positively correlated with district measles incidence (Spearman rho = + 0.370, p = 0.027), consistent with surveillance activity bias. Punjab experienced a measles epidemic peaking in 2024, with 72,565 reported suspected cases and 184 deaths across 2022 to 2026, driven by accumulation of unvaccinated children within districts reporting nominally high MCV1 coverage. The progressive shift in disease burden toward infants under 12 months demands renewed focus on vaccination system quality, zero-dose outreach, and surveillance sensitivity. These findings are relevant to other high-burden EMRO settings facing similar immunization system challenges.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles resurgences following the COVID-19 pandemic have placed millions of unvaccinated children at risk across South Asia.
Design and population
Variables analyzed included case counts, incidence rates per 100,000 population, age at illness onset, sex, vaccination status, case outcomes, and district-level geographic distribution.
Core methods
Statistical analysis used the Cochran-Armitage trend test, Wilson 95% confidence intervals, and Spearman rank correlation.
Main results
Reported measles cases escalated from 6,613 in 2022 to a peak of 23,623 in 2024 (incidence 18.92 per 100,000 population), before declining to 19,913 in 2025 and 9,898 through week 23 of 2026.
Interpretation and novelty
Reported first-dose measles-containing vaccine (MCV1) coverage was paradoxically positively correlated with district measles incidence (Spearman rho = + 0.370, p = 0.027), consistent with surveillance activity bias.
Scientific and public-health significance
The progressive shift in disease burden toward infants under 12 months demands renewed focus on vaccination system quality, zero-dose outreach, and surveillance sensitivity.
Limitations and evidence strength
These findings are relevant to other high-burden EMRO settings facing similar immunization system challenges.

验尸官确认美国 麻疹 死亡,而 CDC 计数在死亡人数争议中仍为零。

Coroner confirms US measles death while CDC count remains at zero amid row over the fatalities.
作者： Chris Baraniuk, Baraniuk C.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
目前,美国 麻疹 的 疫苗接种 比率正在下降。9 雷丁大学病毒学教授伊恩·琼斯 (Ian Jones) 表示,对 麻疹 疫苗 毫无根据的怀疑令人担忧。
设计与对象
他在周末首次向美国媒体证实,麻疹 导致了女孩的死亡。2 此前提到兰卡斯特县“所谓的 麻疹 死亡”3 的美国卫生部长小罗伯特·F·肯尼迪 (Robert F Kennedy Jr) 在社交媒体上承认并似乎接受了这一更新。4 截至周二上午,美国疾病控制与预防中心 (CDC) 仍未更新其有关美国 麻疹 统计数据的网页,将死亡纳入其中。5该页面上的一份声明只是说,目前没有死亡记录表明 麻疹 是“根本原因”。它还补充说,疾病预防控制中心正在制定新的“因 麻疹 死亡的标准化病例定义”。该机构确实表示,今年迄今为止,美国已有 3134 起 麻疹 案件,已明显高于去年的 2289 起案件总数。
核心方法
所提供的证据没有明确说明核心实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果

社交媒体上爆发了关于孩子们的死亡是“麻疹相关”还是“麻疹造成”的争论。但伦敦大学学院名誉副教授、社区儿科医生大卫·埃利曼表示,这只是“文字游戏”。他告诉《英国医学杂志》:“你想知道其中有多少是基于医学,有多少是基于政治。”他认为兰开斯特县的情况导致“对现有机构的信任持续混乱和破坏”。埃利曼指出,由于婴儿太小,无法接种麻疹疫苗,因此保护他们免受这种高度传染性疾病的唯一方法是通过给老年人接种疫苗来建立群体免疫力。

Coroner confirms US measles death while CDC count remains at zero amid row over the fatalities.

Authors: Chris Baraniuk, Baraniuk C.
Original abstract (English)
Coroner confirms US measles death while CDC count remains at zero amid row over the fatalities BMJ 2026; 394 doi: https://doi.org/10.1136/bmj-2026-100789 (Published 08 September 2026) Cite this as: BMJ 2026;394:e100789 A child has been confirmed to have died of measles by the coroner of Lancaster County, Pennsylvania—the first such death in Pennsylvania for 35 years.1 It follows intense scrutiny over the case, in which US health officials sought to downplay reports that two recent infant deaths in the county were caused by the disease. Stephen Diamantoni, Lancaster County coroner, told The BMJ that the death of a six week old girl on 18 August was “caused by measles.” The girl also had Amish lethal microcephaly. “The average life expectancy with this condition is 4 to 6 months of age. Because of the compromised state of these children many die of respiratory infections,” Diamantoni said. He first confirmed to US media at the weekend that measles had caused the girl’s death.2 US health secretary Robert F Kennedy Jr, who previously referred to “so called measles deaths”3 in Lancaster County, acknowledged and appeared to accept the update on social media.4 As of Tuesday morning, however the US Centers for Disease Control & Prevention (CDC) still had not updated its web page on US measles statistics to include the death.5 A statement on that page simply says that there are currently no death records that indicate measles as the “underlying cause.” It adds that the CDC is developing a new “standardised case definition for deaths due to measles.” The agency does say that there have been 3134 measles cases in the US to date this year, already significantly higher than last year’s total of 2289. The BMJ approached the CDC for comment. The Lancaster County coroner’s update is t...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Vaccination rates for measles are currently falling in the US.9 Ian Jones, professor of virology at the University of Reading, said unfounded scepticism around the measles vaccine was a cause for concern.
Design and population
He first confirmed to US media at the weekend that measles had caused the girl’s death.2 US health secretary Robert F Kennedy Jr, who previously referred to “so called measles deaths”3 in Lancaster County, acknowledged and appeared to accept the update on social media.4 As of Tuesday morning, however the US Centers for Disease Control & Prevention (CDC) still had not updated its web page on US measles statistics to include the death.5 A statement on that page simply says that there are currently no death records that indicate measles as the “underlying cause.” It adds that the CDC is developing a new “standardised case definition for deaths due to measles.” The agency does say that there have been 3134 measles cases in the US to date this year, already significantly higher than last year’s total of 2289.
Core methods
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.
Main results
Coroner confirms US measles death while CDC count remains at zero amid row over the fatalities BMJ 2026; 394 doi: https://doi.org/10.1136/bmj-2026-100789 (Published 08 September 2026) Cite this as: BMJ 2026;394:e100789 A child has been confirmed to have died of measles by the coroner of Lancaster County, Pennsylvania—the first such death in Pennsylvania for 35 years.1 It follows intense scrutiny over the case, in which US health officials sought to downplay reports that two recent infant deaths in the county were caused by the disease.
Interpretation and novelty
Arguments have erupted on social media over whether the children’s deaths were “measles associated” or “measles caused.” But David Elliman, a community paediatrician and honorary associate professor at University College London, said this is merely “playing with words.” “You wonder how much of this is based on medicine and how much it is based on politics,” he told The BMJ, arguing that the Lancaster County situation was leading to “continued confusion and destruction of trust in what were established bodies.” Elliman pointed out that, because babies are too young to be immunised against measles, the only means of protecting them from this highly contagious disease was through vaccinating older people to establish herd immunity.
Scientific and public-health significance
“It’s a smouldering situation that is just not getting any better-and that will lead, ultimately, to further deaths,” he said.
Limitations and evidence strength
“We are continuing our investigation as to what may have caused the laceration.” While Diamantoni suggested the case of the newborn boy was one of the “measles associated” deaths cited by the Pennsylvania Department of Health, he added, “I am uncertain whether the death of the 6 week infant is the [second] death that they referred to.” The BMJ has approached the Pennsylvania Department of Health for clarification.

2020年至2025年巴西麻疹住院的流行病学概况

EPIDEMIOLOGICAL PROFILE OF MEASLES HOSPITALIZATIONS IN BRAZIL FROM 2020 TO 2025
作者： Thiago Assis Venâncio, Laís Pires Mendes, Luísa Tavares Justino
摘要中文翻译目的:分析 2020 年至 2025 年巴西因 麻疹 住院的流行病学概况。方法:这是一项生态、描述性、回顾性研究,基于巴西统一卫生系统 (SIH/SUS) 医院信息系统(可通过 DATASUS/TABNET 获取)的二手数据。根据年份、地理区域、性别、种族/民族和年龄组对入院率、死亡率和 死亡率 率进行分析,并将 麻疹 视为主要 死亡率 (ICD-10 B05)。结果:研究期间共有 940 人入院,8 人死亡。2020年至2023年住院人数明显减少,随后2024年至2025年略有增加。北部地区住院人数比例最高(58.62%),其中1岁以下儿童住院人数最多,其次是1至4岁儿童。在 80 岁或以上的个体中观察到 死亡率 比例最高。住院治疗在性别之间的分布也类似,在现有信息的记录中,棕色人种/族裔类别占主导地位。结论:研究期间,麻疹 相关住院人数大幅减少;然而,住院治疗的持续存在和近年来观察到的增加凸显了巴西需要保持高 疫苗接种 覆盖率、持续的流行病学 监测、及时的 诊断 以及对医疗保健专业人员的持续培训,以防止巴西病毒重新引入和持续的 传播。

问题与背景
分析 2020 年至 2025 年巴西因 麻疹 住院的流行病学概况。
设计与对象
研究期间共有 940 人入院,8 人死亡。
核心方法
这是一项生态性、描述性、回顾性研究,基于巴西统一卫生系统 (SIH/SUS) 医院信息系统的二手数据,可通过 DATASUS/TABNET 获取。
主要结果
2020 年至 2023 年住院人数显着减少,随后 2024 年和 2025 年略有增加。
解释与创新
住院治疗在性别之间的分布也类似,在现有信息的记录中,棕色人种/族裔类别占主导地位。
科研与公卫意义
研究期间,麻疹 相关的住院人数大幅减少;然而,住院治疗的持续存在和近年来观察到的增加凸显了巴西需要保持高 疫苗接种 覆盖率、持续的流行病学 监测、及时的 诊断 以及对医疗保健专业人员的持续培训,以防止巴西病毒重新引入和持续的 传播。

EPIDEMIOLOGICAL PROFILE OF MEASLES HOSPITALIZATIONS IN BRAZIL FROM 2020 TO 2025

Authors: Thiago Assis Venâncio, Laís Pires Mendes, Luísa Tavares Justino
Original abstract (English)
Objective: To analyze the epidemiological profile of hospital admissions due to measles in Brazil from 2020 to 2025. Methods: This was an ecological, descriptive, retrospective study based on secondary data from the Hospital Information System of the Brazilian Unified Health System (SIH/SUS), available through DATASUS/TABNET. Hospital admissions, deaths, and mortality rates were analyzed according to year, geographic region, sex, race/ethnicity, and age group, considering measles as the primary diagnosis (ICD-10 B05). Results: A total of 940 hospital admissions and eight deaths were recorded during the study period. A marked reduction in hospital admissions was observed between 2020 and 2023, followed by a slight increase in 2024 and 2025. The North region accounted for the highest proportion of hospitalizations (58.62%), while children under 1 year of age had the highest number of admissions, followed by those aged 1 to 4 years. The highest proportional mortality was observed among individuals aged 80 years or older. Hospitalizations were similarly distributed between sexes, with a predominance of the brown race/ethnicity category among records with available information. Conclusion: Measles-related hospital admissions decreased substantially during the study period; however, the persistence of hospitalizations and the increase observed in recent years highlight the need to maintain high vaccination coverage, continuous epidemiological surveillance, timely diagnosis, and ongoing training of healthcare professionals to prevent viral reintroduction and sustained transmission in Brazil.

Core methods

Main results

Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

具有不完整 疫苗接种 和 1 个免疫力减弱的 麻疹 的 SVIR 模型分析:Sidoarjo-Indonesia 案例

SVIR Model Analysis of Measles with Incomplete Vaccination and 1 Waning Immunity: Sidoarjo-Indonesia Case
作者： Ainishafa Ainishafa, Abadi Abadi, Dian Savitri
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
使用下一代矩阵方法推导出基本繁殖数R0,并通过Lyapunov函数严格证明了无病平衡和地方病平衡的全局稳定性。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
本研究开发并分析了印度尼西亚诗都阿佐市麻疹 传播 的易感-疫苗接种-感染-康复 (SVIR) 区室模型,其中考虑了免疫力的减弱。
主要结果
模型参数是根据西多阿佐市 2023 年每周的 监测 数据使用粒子群优化 (PSO)、扩展卡尔曼滤波器、最大似然估计和最小二乘法进行估计,并通过平均绝对百分比误差 (MAPE) 进行评估。PSO 实现了最低的 MAPE (11.17%),优于其他方法。
解释与创新
估计的 R0 = 0.668 确认无病状态。
科研与公卫意义
麻疹 在印度尼西亚仍然是一个重大的公共卫生挑战,特别是在 麻疹 覆盖范围不均匀且免疫力下降的地区。
局限与证据强度
Normalized sensitivity analysis identifies vaccineefficacy as the most influential parameter; a decline in efficacy to0 .75elevates R0 close tothe critical threshold, and any further reduction pushes the system into an endemic state.These findings underscore the necessity of maintaining high vaccine efficacy through boosterprograms and strengthened immunization strategies to sustain measles elimination at thedistrict level.

SVIR Model Analysis of Measles with Incomplete Vaccination and 1 Waning Immunity: Sidoarjo-Indonesia Case

Authors: Ainishafa Ainishafa, Abadi Abadi, Dian Savitri
Original abstract (English)
Measles remains a significant public health challenge in Indonesia, especially in regionswith heterogeneous vaccination coverage and waning immunity. This study develops andanalyzes a Susceptible–Vaccinated–Infected–Recovered (SVIR) compartmental model formeasles transmission in Sidoarjo City, Indonesia, incorporating waning immunity. The basicreproduction number R0 is derived using the next-generation matrix method, and the globalstability of both the disease-free and endemic equilibria is rigorously proven via Lyapunovfunctions. Model parameters are estimated from weekly 2023 surveillance data in Sidoarjo Cityusing Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO), Extended Kalman Filter, Maximum LikelihoodEstimation, and Least Squares, and evaluated via Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE).PSO achieves the lowest MAPE (11.17%), outperforming the other methods. The estimatedR0 = 0.668confirms a disease-free regime. Normalized sensitivity analysis identifies vaccineefficacy as the most influential parameter; a decline in efficacy to0 .75elevates R0 close tothe critical threshold, and any further reduction pushes the system into an endemic state.These findings underscore the necessity of maintaining high vaccine efficacy through boosterprograms and strengthened immunization strategies to sustain measles elimination at thedistrict level.

流行病学参数不确定下双剂量麻疹模糊数学模型

Fuzzy based Mathematical Model of Measles with Double-Dose Vaccination under Epidemiological Parameter Uncertainty
作者： Barikisu Abu, Helen Olaronke Edogbanya, Babarinsa Olayiwola
摘要中文翻译本研究开发了一种基于模糊的数学模型,用于在流行病学参数不确定性下结合双剂量 疫苗接种 和 治疗 的 麻疹 动态。该模型通过将选定的参数(包括 传播、疫苗接种、治疗 和恢复率)表示为三角模糊数,扩展了确定性 麻疹 框架。建立了模型的基本性质,并推导了无病平衡和基本再生数。采用$r$-cut方法来获得不确定参数和模糊基本再生数的区间值表示。结果表明,随着$r$切割水平的增加,再生数的不确定性区间减小,并在$r=1$时收敛到确定性再生数。干预分析进一步表明,疾病 传播 的减少与增加的 疫苗接种 覆盖率和 治疗 的组合可以减少确定性繁殖数和低于统一的模糊繁殖数的上限。因此,所提出的框架为评估 麻疹 传播 和在流行病学参数不精确已知时进行控制提供了有用的方法。

核心方法
建立了模型的基本性质,并推导了无病平衡和基本再生数。
主要结果
干预分析进一步表明,疾病 传播 的减少与增加的 疫苗接种 覆盖率和 治疗 的组合可以减少确定性繁殖数和低于统一的模糊繁殖数的上限。

结果表明,随着$r$切割水平的增加,再生数的不确定性区间减小,并在$r=1$时收敛到确定性再生数。

Fuzzy based Mathematical Model of Measles with Double-Dose Vaccination under Epidemiological Parameter Uncertainty

Authors: Barikisu Abu, Helen Olaronke Edogbanya, Babarinsa Olayiwola
Original abstract (English)
This study develops a fuzzy-based mathematical model for the transmission dynamics of measles incorporating double-dose vaccination and treatment under epidemiological parameter uncertainty. The model extends a deterministic measles framework by representing selected parameters, including the transmission, vaccination, treatment and recovery rates, as triangular fuzzy numbers. Basic properties of the model are established, and the disease-free equilibrium and basic reproduction number are derived. The $r$-cut approach is employed to obtain interval-valued representations of the uncertain parameters and the fuzzy basic reproduction number. The results show that the uncertainty interval of the reproduction number decreases as the $r$-cut level increases and converges to the deterministic reproduction number at $r=1$. Intervention analysis further shows that a combined reduction in disease transmission with increased vaccination coverage and treatment can reduce both the deterministic reproduction number and the upper bound of the fuzzy reproduction number below unity. The proposed framework therefore provides a useful approach for assessing measles transmission and control when epidemiological parameters are imprecisely known.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The $r$-cut approach is employed to obtain interval-valued representations of the uncertain parameters and the fuzzy basic reproduction number.
Design and population
This study develops a fuzzy-based mathematical model for the transmission dynamics of measles incorporating double-dose vaccination and treatment under epidemiological parameter uncertainty.
Core methods
Basic properties of the model are established, and the disease-free equilibrium and basic reproduction number are derived.
Main results
Intervention analysis further shows that a combined reduction in disease transmission with increased vaccination coverage and treatment can reduce both the deterministic reproduction number and the upper bound of the fuzzy reproduction number below unity.
Interpretation and novelty
The results show that the uncertainty interval of the reproduction number decreases as the $r$-cut level increases and converges to the deterministic reproduction number at $r=1$.
Scientific and public-health significance
The proposed framework therefore provides a useful approach for assessing measles transmission and control when epidemiological parameters are imprecisely known.
Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on abstract only. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹 - 澳大利亚健康科学学生在童年后的免疫力 麻疹 的持续性

Measles, Mumps, and Rubella - Persistence of Immunity After Childhood Vaccination in Australian Health Science Students

Authors: Christine S. Schoenfisch
Original abstract (English)
Measles, Mumps, and Rubella (MMR) continue to pose global public health risks, and immunity among undergraduate health science students is essential to ensure safe clinical placement and workforce readiness. Despite high childhood vaccination coverage, emerging evidence suggests that some young adults may have incomplete, waning, or uncertain immunity. This program of research aimed to evaluate the long-term effectiveness of the MMR vaccine from childhood through to adulthood among undergraduate health science students. Three studies explored biological, behavioural, and system-level contributors to immunity gaps. Paper 1 used a scoping review methodology to map how the nurse practitioner (NP) immuniser role is conceptualised within primary healthcare (PHC). The review found that although NPs frequently deliver vaccination-related care, their role in immunisation assessment and follow-up is under-recognised, revealing opportunities for NPled models to address barriers and improve vaccine communication. Paper 2 used a quantitative cohort study, using the Vaccine Effectiveness Framework, to compare Australian Immunisation Register (AIR) records with serological testing among domestic and international students. Discrepancies were identified between documented vaccination and actual immune status, including students with complete AIR records who lacked serological immunity, providing evidence of waning immunity and limitations of relying solely on administrative records. Paper 3 used a quantitative cross-sectional survey informed by the Health Belief Model (HBM) to examine students’ knowledge, attitudes, and beliefs about MMR diseases, vaccination, and immunity testing. Knowledge gaps, low perceived susceptibility, misconceptions about lifelong immunity, and difficulty interpreting vaccination records were strongly associated with reduced intention to check immunity, highlighting the need for structured education within health science programs. Together, these studies demonstrate that immunity gaps arise from the intersection of biological waning immunity, incomplete or unreliable records, and behavioural influences on vaccine decisionmaking. This program of research fills a critical gap by integrating biological, behavioural, and system level evidence and demonstrates the potential for NP-led immunisation models to strengthen assessment, communication, and follow-up in primary healthcare. The findings offer practical strategies to improve vaccine literacy, immunity monitoring, and long-term immunity among future healthcare professionals.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This program of research aimed to evaluate the long-term effectiveness of the MMR vaccine from childhood through to adulthood among undergraduate health science students.
Design and population
Paper 2 used a quantitative cohort study, using the Vaccine Effectiveness Framework, to compare Australian Immunisation Register (AIR) records with serological testing among domestic and international students.
Core methods
Paper 3 used a quantitative cross-sectional survey informed by the Health Belief Model (HBM) to examine students’ knowledge, attitudes, and beliefs about MMR diseases, vaccination, and immunity testing.
Main results
Discrepancies were identified between documented vaccination and actual immune status, including students with complete AIR records who lacked serological immunity, providing evidence of waning immunity and limitations of relying solely on administrative records.
Interpretation and novelty
Together, these studies demonstrate that immunity gaps arise from the intersection of biological waning immunity, incomplete or unreliable records, and behavioural influences on vaccine decisionmaking.
Scientific and public-health significance
Knowledge gaps, low perceived susceptibility, misconceptions about lifelong immunity, and difficulty interpreting vaccination records were strongly associated with reduced intention to check immunity, highlighting the need for structured education within health science programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
Despite high childhood vaccination coverage, emerging evidence suggests that some young adults may have incomplete, waning, or uncertain immunity.

2026 年孟加拉国 麻疹 暴发:对免疫差距和护理缺陷的批评

The 2026 Measles Outbreak in Bangladesh: A Critique of Immunity Gaps and Care Deficiencies
作者： Syed Abu Hasanth
摘要中文翻译孟加拉国在 5 月中旬达到顶峰的严重 麻疹 麻疹 中进入 2026 年。本文分析了公共卫生部门对 暴发 的反应,并评估了政府对危机的管理。卫生专业人员和 麻疹 专家将 麻疹 归因于常规免疫积累的差距、公共卫生基础设施薄弱、儿童营养状况不佳、维生素 A 普遍缺乏以及孕产妇在怀孕和哺乳期间营养不良。

设计与对象
该研究评估了孟加拉国对2026年麻疹 暴发的公共卫生应对措施,重点关注政府对危机的管理。
核心方法
卫生专业人员和 麻疹 专家将 麻疹 归因于常规免疫积累的差距、公共卫生基础设施薄弱、儿童营养状况不佳、维生素 A 普遍缺乏以及孕产妇在怀孕和哺乳期间营养不良。

The 2026 Measles Outbreak in Bangladesh: A Critique of Immunity Gaps and Care Deficiencies

Authors: Syed Abu Hasanth
Original abstract (English)
Bangladesh entered 2026 amid a severe measles outbreak that peaked in mid-May. This article analyses the public health response to the outbreak and evaluates the government's management of the crisis. Health professionals and measles experts have attributed the outbreak to accumulated gaps in routine immunisation, weaknesses in public health infrastructure, poor nutritional status among children, widespread vitamin A deficiency, and maternal undernutrition during pregnancy and breastfeeding.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Bangladesh entered 2026 amid a severe measles outbreak that peaked in mid-May, prompting an analysis of the public health response and government management of the crisis.
Design and population
The study evaluates the public health response to the 2026 measles outbreak in Bangladesh, focusing on the government's management of the crisis.
Core methods
Health professionals and measles experts attributed the outbreak to accumulated gaps in routine immunisation, weaknesses in public health infrastructure, poor nutritional status among children, widespread vitamin A deficiency, and maternal undernutrition during pregnancy and breastfeeding.
Main results
The study identifies accumulated gaps in routine immunisation, weaknesses in public health infrastructure, poor nutritional status among children, widespread vitamin A deficiency, and maternal undernutrition during pregnancy and breastfeeding as contributing factors to the outbreak.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the outbreak as a result of systemic failures in immunisation and public health infrastructure, highlighting the novel intersection of nutritional deficiencies and measles vulnerability in the Bangladeshi context.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for strengthened routine immunisation programmes, improved public health infrastructure, and targeted nutritional interventions to prevent future measles outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study relies on expert attributions without primary data collection or statistical analysis, limiting causal inference.

问题与背景
Studies conducted in resource-limited countries in the 1980s and 1990s reported that high doses of vitamin A administered orally in 2 daily doses reduced the risk of mortality from measles in children under 2 years of age and shortened the duration of diarrhea and fever by 2 and 1 days, respectively.15 The World Health Organiza- tion (WHO) recommends children diagnosed with measles be given 2 doses of vitamin A supplements.
设计与对象
Common complications, occurring in approximately 1 in 10 cases, include otitis media, pneumonia, conjunctivitis, and diarrhea.4,10 Less common but severe complications include respiratory failure and encephalitis, which occur at a rate of 1 in 1000.4,8,10 Encephalitis can occur during the first 7 days of infection, 1 to 6 months after infection, or even years after full recovery.9 Death can occur, but it is rare (rate of 1 to 10 in 10,000 cases of measles) and is mainly due to neurologic or respiratory-related complications.11 Measles infection can lead to a number of long-term sequelae,4,10 including blindness, deafness, and permanent neurological sequelae.

为了确认 诊断,除了 血清学 之外,还需要 2 份临床标本(鼻咽或咽喉拭子)和尿液进行聚合酶链反应(PCR)病毒检测。

Measles: A resurgent threat and the role of pharmacists in prevention

Authors: Marra F, Carr R
Original abstract (English)
SPECIAL CLINICAL REVIEW PEER-REVIEWED Measles: A resurgent threat and the role of pharmacists in prevention Fawziah Marra BSc(Pharm), PharmD, FCSHP, ACPR, RPh1, Roxane Carr BSc(Pharm), PharmD, FCSHP, ACPR, RPh1,2 Overview Considered eliminated in Canada over 25 years ago, measles has re-emerged as a public health concern due to declining vaccination rates and increased global travel. Epidemiology The measles virus is a member of the Paramyxoviridae family, which includes mumps, parainfluenza, and respiratory syncy- tial (RSV) viruses.2 Infection with measles results in lifelong immunity.2 Measles is a nationally notifiable disease.3 The live- attenuated vaccine, containing measles only, was introduced into Canadian routine immunization programs in the early 1970s.4 Between 1974 and 1983, a 1-dose combined vaccine for measles, mumps, and rubella (MMR) was introduced. 32 C P J / R P C r J A N U A RY / F E B R U A RY 2 0 2 6 r V O L 1 5 9 , N O 1 DOI:10.3138/cpj-25-0104 SPECIAL CLINICAL REVIEW FIGURE 1 Number of reported measles cases in Canada, 1998 to 20234 FIGURE 2 Geographic distribution of total measles cases by provinces and territories, 2025 after an infected person has left the area.2,8 Transmission oc- curs through inhalation of infectious droplets, direct contact with respiratory secretions, and activities such as sharing food, drinks, products that produce smoke or vapours, or kissing someone with measles.4,8 Individuals with measles are infec- tious from 4 days before until 4 days after the appearance of the rash.8 The secondary attack rate among susceptible contacts is ≥90%. In fact, recently Canada, and by extension all the Americas, has been stripped of its measles-free certification by the Pan American Health Organization (PAHO) after failing to curb an ou...

Question and background
Studies conducted in resource-limited countries in the 1980s and 1990s reported that high doses of vitamin A administered orally in 2 daily doses reduced the risk of mortality from measles in children under 2 years of age and shortened the duration of diarrhea and fever by 2 and 1 days, respectively.15 The World Health Organiza- tion (WHO) recommends children diagnosed with measles be given 2 doses of vitamin A supplements.
Design and population
Common complications, occurring in approximately 1 in 10 cases, include otitis media, pneumonia, conjunctivitis, and diarrhea.4,10 Less common but severe complications include respiratory failure and encephalitis, which occur at a rate of 1 in 1000.4,8,10 Encephalitis can occur during the first 7 days of infection, 1 to 6 months after infection, or even years after full recovery.9 Death can occur, but it is rare (rate of 1 to 10 in 10,000 cases of measles) and is mainly due to neurologic or respiratory-related complications.11 Measles infection can lead to a number of long-term sequelae,4,10 including blindness, deafness, and permanent neurological sequelae.
Core methods
To confirm the diagnosis, 2 clinical specimens, either a nasopha- ryngeal or a throat swab, and urine are required for viral detection by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) in addition to serology.
Main results
In coun- tries where measles is endemic, that is, low- to middle-income countries, the first dose is usually given at 9 months of age (can be as early as 6 months).17 In countries with low rates of measles infections, the first dose is given at 12 months of age, as this is the age when maternal antibody levels in the infant will have decreased to a low enough level to optimize the infant’s development of antibodies from the vaccine.
Interpretation and novelty
The local reactions of pain, swelling, redness at the injec- tion site, and fever are more common with the MMRV vaccine compared to the MMR vaccine, occurring in 10% of children.

局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取基于检索到的全文证据。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

[国际旅行后发烧和皮疹:疫苗失败的两例]。

[Fever and rash after international travel: two cases of vaccine failure].
作者： Annika P. Schnell, Luisa Berckenhagen, Stefan Schmiedel
摘要中文翻译德国的 麻疹 病例主要是输入病例或与输入相关的病例。报告了两名出现 麻疹 疫苗 失败的回国旅行者:一名 49 岁男性,在单剂 麻疹 后出现原发性 疫苗 失败,随后出现严重的 麻疹 肺炎;以及一名 22 岁男性,在疑似免疫缺陷的情况下出现继发性 疫苗 失败,病程轻微,且病情严重。非典型疹。即使在接种疫苗的个体中,麻疹 仍然是相关的差异 麻疹,旅行医学咨询应常规包括对 麻疹 疫苗接种 状态的评估。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
德国的 麻疹 病例主要是输入病例或与输入相关的病例。该研究报告了两例返程旅客中 麻疹 疫苗 失败的案例。
设计与对象
给出了返回旅行者中麻疹 疫苗 的两个病例报告 失败。一个病例涉及一名 49 岁男性,在单剂量 疫苗接种 后出现原发性 疫苗 失败,另一例涉及一名 22 岁男性,在疑似免疫缺陷的情况下出现继发性 疫苗 失败。
核心方法
该研究描述了两个病例的临床表现和 疫苗接种 病史。它不提供详细的实验室或统计方法。
主要结果
两例均为麻疹 疫苗失败:一例为重症肺炎,另一例病程较轻,出疹不典型。
解释与创新
作者强调,即使在接种疫苗的个体中,麻疹 仍然是相关的差异 诊断,强调在旅行医学咨询中评估 疫苗接种 状态的重要性。

研究结果强调,在接种疫苗的个体中诊断 麻疹 时需要保持警惕,并建议在旅行医学咨询期间对 麻疹 麻疹 状态进行常规评估。

该研究仅限于 病例报告,可能无法提供具有普适性的结果。该证据基于临床观察,强度中等。

[Fever and rash after international travel: two cases of vaccine failure].

Authors: Annika P. Schnell, Luisa Berckenhagen, Stefan Schmiedel
Original abstract (English)
Measles cases in Germany are predominantly imported or import-associated. Two returning travelers with measles vaccine failure are reported: a 49-year-old male with primary vaccine failure after single-dose vaccination who developed severe measles pneumonitis, and a 22-year-old male with secondary vaccine failure in the context of suspected immunodeficiency who presented with a mild course and atypical exanthem. Measles remains a relevant differential diagnosis even in vaccinated individuals, and travel medicine consultations should routinely include an assessment of measles vaccination status.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles cases in Germany are predominantly imported or import-associated. The study reports two cases of measles vaccine failure in returning travelers.
Design and population
Two case reports of measles vaccine failure in returning travelers are presented. One case involves a 49-year-old male with primary vaccine failure after single-dose vaccination, and the other involves a 22-year-old male with secondary vaccine failure in the context of suspected immunodeficiency.
Core methods
The study describes the clinical presentation and vaccination history of the two cases. It does not provide detailed laboratory or statistical methods.
Main results
The two cases involved measles vaccine failure: one with severe pneumonitis and the other with a mild course and atypical exanthem.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors highlight that measles remains a relevant differential diagnosis even in vaccinated individuals, emphasizing the importance of assessing vaccination status in travel medicine consultations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the need for vigilance in diagnosing measles in vaccinated individuals and suggest routine assessment of measles vaccination status during travel medicine consultations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to case reports, which may not provide generalizable results. The evidence is based on clinical observations and is considered moderate in strength.

时间、地点、人物:作为政治实践的领域流行病学。

Time, place, person: Field epidemiology as a political practice.
作者： Luisa Enria
摘要中文翻译埃博拉疫情结束五年后,塞拉利昂的一个地区收到了 麻疹 警报。本文讲述了随后发生的流行病的故事以及在管理突发卫生事件的新的全球流行病防范和生物安全范式背景下采取的应对措施。关注构成 暴发 调查的日常遭遇以及这些互动中出现的叙述、谈判和争论,使我们能够将现场 流行病学 想象为一种政治实践。这意味着了解全球流行病学逻辑如何与当地政治和排斥形式相融合。努力让人们了解流行病并对其做出反应,然后重塑政治关系,治理公民并秩序化公共卫生治理层面之间的关系。同时,现场流行病学工作与地方政治谈判的纠缠,也产生了新的争论、抵抗和修复的空间。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
埃博拉疫情结束五年后,塞拉利昂的一个地区收到了 麻疹 警报。本文探讨了突发卫生事件背景下 流行病学 领域的政治维度。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注《暴发》调查中的日常遭遇以及这些互动中产生的叙述、谈判和争论。它研究了 流行病学 领域如何在新的全球流行病防范和生物安全范式中运作。
核心方法
这些方法包括分析 暴发 调查期间出现的相互作用和叙述,强调全球流行病学逻辑如何与当地政治动态相交叉。
主要结果
该研究表明,让人们了解流行病并应对流行病的努力重塑了政治关系,影响着公民的治理方式以及公共卫生治理水平的互动方式。
解释与创新
作者将 流行病学 领域解释为一种重塑治理结构和公民关系的政治实践,为全球卫生范式与地方政治的交叉提供了新颖的视角。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现有助于理解 流行病学 领域如何成为塑造政治关系和公共卫生治理的工具,并对未来的卫生应急响应产生影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于摘要,没有提供详细的经验数据,限制了证据的强度。然而,它提供了一个基于实地观察的概念框架。

Time, place, person: Field epidemiology as a political practice.

Authors: Luisa Enria
Original abstract (English)
Five years after the end of the Ebola epidemic, a district in Sierra Leone received a measles alert. This article tells the story of the epidemic that followed and the response that was mounted in the context of the new global epidemic preparedness and biosecurity paradigms that govern the management of health emergencies. Focusing on the daily encounters that constitute outbreak investigations and the narratives, negotiations and contestations that emerge from these interactions, allows us to envision field epidemiology as a political practice. This means seeing how global epidemiological logics converge with local politics and forms of exclusion. Efforts to make epidemics knowable and responding to them, then, reshape political relations, governing citizens and ordering relations between levels of public health governance. At the same time, as field epidemiological work becomes entangled in local political negotiations, it also generates new spaces for contestation, resistance and repair.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Five years after the end of the Ebola epidemic, a district in Sierra Leone received a measles alert. This article explores the political dimensions of field epidemiology in the context of health emergencies.
Design and population
The study focuses on the daily encounters in outbreak investigations and the narratives, negotiations, and contestations that arise from these interactions. It examines how field epidemiology operates within the new global epidemic preparedness and biosecurity paradigms.
Core methods
The methods involve analyzing the interactions and narratives that emerge during outbreak investigations, highlighting how global epidemiological logics intersect with local political dynamics.
Main results
The study reveals that efforts to make epidemics knowable and respond to them reshape political relations, influencing how citizens are governed and how public health governance levels interact.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret field epidemiology as a political practice that reshapes governance structures and citizen relations, offering a novel perspective on the intersection of global health paradigms and local politics.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the understanding of how field epidemiology can be a tool for shaping political relations and public health governance, with implications for future health emergency responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on an abstract and does not provide detailed empirical data, limiting the strength of the evidence. However, it offers a conceptual framework grounded in field observations.

The Relationship Between Political Affiliation, Perceptions of Science and Scientists, and Perceived Safety and Effectiveness of the MMR, Flu, and COVID-19 Vaccines: United States, 2024.

Authors: Dror Walter, Yotam Ophir, Patrick E Jamieson, Kathleen Hall-Jamieson
Original abstract (English)
To examine the associations between political party identification, perceptions of science and scientists, and perceived safety and effectiveness of the measles, mumps, and rubella, flu, and COVID-19 vaccines. We used multiple regression analyses to examine survey data collected in November 2024 from a national probability sample of 1763 US adults. We measured perceptions of science and scientists using the Factors Assessing Science's Self-Presentation (FASS) scale, which assesses beliefs that scientists are credible, prudent, and unbiased and that science is beneficial and self-correcting. Respondents perceived the measles, mumps, and rubella vaccine as the safest and most effective (mean safety score = 3.54 [SD = 0.71]; mean effectiveness score = 3.63 [SD = 0.68]) and the COVID-19 vaccine as the least safe and effective (mean safety score = 2.98 [SD = 1.07]; mean effectiveness score = 2.81 [SD = 1.02]). After adjustment for FASS factors, political party identification was associated only with perceptions of the COVID-19 vaccine (P < .001). Associations between political party identification and FASS factors varied by vaccine type. Perceiving science as beneficial was the factor most consistently associated with vaccine evaluations. Republicans are more concerned about the safety and effectiveness of vaccines, with politicization most evident for the COVID-19 vaccine. Public Health Implications. These findings can guide tailored communication strategies addressing vaccine safety and effectiveness concerns among specific audiences. (Am J Public Health. Published online ahead of print September 10, 2026:e1-e9. https://doi.org/10.2105/AJPH.2026.308606).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
To examine the associations between political party identification, perceptions of science and scientists, and perceived safety and effectiveness of the measles, mumps, and rubella, flu, and COVID-19 vaccines.
Design and population
We used multiple regression analyses to examine survey data collected in November 2024 from a national probability sample of 1763 US adults.
Core methods
We measured perceptions of science and scientists using the Factors Assessing Science's Self-Presentation (FASS) scale, which assesses beliefs that scientists are credible, prudent, and unbiased and that science is beneficial and self-correcting.
Main results
Respondents perceived the measles, mumps, and rubella vaccine as the safest and most effective (mean safety score = 3.54 [SD = 0.71]; mean effectiveness score = 3.63 [SD = 0.68]) and the COVID-19 vaccine as the least safe and effective (mean safety score = 2.98 [SD = 1.07]; mean effectiveness score = 2.81 [SD = 1.02]).
Interpretation and novelty
After adjustment for FASS factors, political party identification was associated only with perceptions of the COVID-19 vaccine (P < .001).
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can guide tailored communication strategies addressing vaccine safety and effectiveness concerns among specific audiences.
Limitations and evidence strength
Perceiving science as beneficial was the factor most consistently associated with vaccine evaluations.

传染病流行的关键性碎片化

Fragmented criticality of infectious disease epidemics
作者： Boxuan Wang, Eugenio Valdano
摘要中文翻译人群对传染病流行的高度易感性通常通过系统级指标(如流行阈值)来总结:一个单一的关键边界。然而,传播在不同社区、宿主群体和传播路径中表现出异质性,公共卫生决策往往需要确定哪些系统部分变得易感,以及在何种条件下发生。本文展示流行关键性本身可能在人口结构中碎片化。通过多类型分支过程,我们识别出决定暴发最终灭绝规模的奇点,并表明人口群体之间的耦合会将各自的阈值转化为复数关键点。这些关键点的实部确定了沿传播力轴的关键变化,虚部决定了其强度和模糊性,而其模式则识别了涉及的亚群体。在意大利,该框架揭示了对呼吸道病原体出现的局部易感性,并在基于输入的策略之上改进了疫苗的分配。对于麻疹在德克萨斯州的分析,它识别出比标准行政或都市划分更同质化的空间单位。在一项与畜牧业相关的耐甲氧西林金黄色葡萄球菌(MRSA)的One Health模型中,它区分了职业性和人-动物传播传播路径。因此,流行病的易感性是由一个结构化的关键景观组织的,而不是单一的阈值。解析这一景观可以直接为公共卫生风险评估和干预提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在理解人群对传染病流行的高度易感性是如何结构化的,超越传统的单一流行阈值,以考虑传播在不同社区、宿主群体和路径中的异质性。研究问题聚焦于流行关键性是否可以在人口结构中碎片化。
设计与对象
本研究采用理论框架,利用多类型分支过程来分析不同人口结构中的流行关键性。它以德克萨斯州、意大利以及与畜牧业相关的MRSA作为案例研究,突出了该框架在不同情境下的应用。
核心方法
作者使用多类型分支过程来建模并识别决定暴发预期规模的奇点。他们分析这些模型,以确定人口群体之间的耦合如何将个体阈值转化为复数关键点,其中实部和虚部分别代表易感性的不同方面。
主要结果
该框架确定了德克萨斯州在 暴发 负担方面比标准行政或大都市分区更均匀的空间单元。在意大利,它揭示了呼吸道病原体出现的局部脆弱性,并展示了相对于基于进口的分配策略的改进 疫苗 分配策略。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为流行病易感性由一个结构化的关键景观组织的证据。这种方法为公共卫生风险评估和干预提供了一种新颖的途径,通过解析关键性在人口亚群体中的复杂动态。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为理解和管理传染病风险提供了新的视角,通过识别局部易感性并改进针对性干预措施(如疫苗的分配),可以增强监测和公共卫生响应策略。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于理论建模和案例研究,可能无法完全反映现实世界的复杂性。然而,该框架得到了明确的词汇证据支持,并应用于特定情境,其推论具有中等程度的可信度。

Fragmented criticality of infectious disease epidemics

Authors: Boxuan Wang, Eugenio Valdano
Original abstract (English)
Population vulnerability to an infectious disease epidemic is commonly summarized by system-level indicators such as the epidemic threshold: a single critical boundary. Yet transmission is heterogeneous across communities, host groups and transmission pathways, and public-health decisions often require identifying which parts of the system become vulnerable, and under which conditions. Here we show that epidemic criticality can itself be fragmented across population structure. Using multitype branching processes, we identify singularities governing the expected size of outbreaks that ultimately become extinct and show that coupling between population strata transforms their individual thresholds into complex-valued critical points. Their real parts locate critical changes along the transmissibility axis, their imaginary parts determine their strength and smearing, and their modes identify the subpopulations involved. In Italy, this framework reveals localized vulnerability to respiratory-pathogen emergence and improves vaccine allocation over importation-based strategies. For measles in Texas, it identifies spatial units more homogeneous in observed outbreak burden than standard administrative or metropolitan partitions. In a One Health model of livestock-associated MRSA, it separates occupational and human--animal transmission pathways. Epidemic vulnerability is therefore organized by a structured critical landscape rather than a single threshold, and resolving this landscape can directly inform public-health risk assessment and intervention.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need to understand how population vulnerability to infectious disease epidemics is structured, moving beyond the traditional single epidemic threshold to account for heterogeneity in transmission across communities, host groups and pathways. The research question focuses on whether epidemic criticality can be fragmented across population structure.
Design and population
The study employs a theoretical framework using multitype branching processes to analyze epidemic criticality across different population structures. It examines measles in Texas, Italy and livestock-associated MRSA as case studies, highlighting the application of this framework to diverse contexts.
Core methods
The authors use multitype branching processes to model and identify singularities that govern the expected size of outbreaks. They analyze these models to determine how coupling between population strata transforms individual thresholds into complex-valued critical points, with real and imaginary parts representing different aspects of vulnerability.
Main results
The framework identifies spatial units in Texas that are more homogeneous in outbreak burden than standard administrative or metropolitan partitions. In Italy, it reveals localized vulnerability to respiratory-pathogen emergence and demonstrates improved vaccine allocation strategies over importation-based ones.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret their findings as evidence that epidemic vulnerability is organized by a structured critical landscape rather than a single threshold. This approach provides a novel method for public-health risk assessment and intervention by resolving the complex dynamics of criticality across population subgroups.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer a new perspective for understanding and managing infectious disease risks by identifying localized vulnerabilities and improving targeted interventions such as vaccine allocation. This could enhance surveillance and response strategies in public health.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to theoretical modeling and case studies, which may not fully capture real-world complexities. However, the framework is supported by explicit lexical evidence and applied to specific contexts, providing a moderate level of confidence in its implications.

高第二年疫苗接种覆盖率与持续的新疫苗疫苗犹豫并存:马拉维三个地区的混合方法研究

High Second Year Immunisation Coverage Alongside Persistent Newer Vaccine Hesitancy: A Mixed-Methods Study in Three Districts of Malawi
作者： Beverly Shaila Laher, Jacqueline Rose Chinkonde, Esther Malata, Susan Barnaba, Jimmy Banda, Hannah Lungu, Beatrice Mkomadzinja, Blessings H Likoswe, Andrew Matchado, Humphrey Chatenga
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估马拉维三个地区儿童在12个月大之后应接种的疫苗完成情况以及与完成率相关的因素,以填补撒哈拉以南非洲地区第二年疫苗接种率决定因素的知识空白。
设计与对象
对奇夸瓦、卡松古和曼戈切的 1,177 名护理人员进行了混合方法横断面调查,以评估家庭财富、粮食不安全和 疫苗接种 状况。
核心方法
该研究使用逻辑回归来检查关联性,并进行焦点小组讨论和关键知情人访谈来解释调查结果。
主要结果
在 1,287 名符合年龄条件的儿童中,94.2% 完成了第二年 疫苗,覆盖范围从 麻疹-风疹 疫苗 的 94% 到人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 的 61%。卫生工作者提醒与 疫苗 的完成相关,而有关 HPV 疫苗 的不孕问题是最常报告的问题。
解释与创新
本研究发现第二年的疫苗接种覆盖率高且公平,卫生工作者的提醒是完成率的一个可修改相关因素。主要的新发现是卫生工作者的提醒与疫苗完成率之间的关联。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为监测和疫苗接种策略提供信息,特别是在解决疫苗犹豫和确保公平覆盖率方面。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其横断面设计以及回忆偏差的潜在影响。由于研究的观察性质,证据强度中等。

High Second Year Immunisation Coverage Alongside Persistent Newer Vaccine Hesitancy: A Mixed-Methods Study in Three Districts of Malawi

Authors: Beverly Shaila Laher, Jacqueline Rose Chinkonde, Esther Malata, Susan Barnaba, Jimmy Banda, Hannah Lungu, Beatrice Mkomadzinja, Blessings H Likoswe, Andrew Matchado, Humphrey Chatenga
Original abstract (English)
Background: /Objectives: The Immunisation Agenda 2030 places sustained coverage and vaccine confidence at the centre of immunisation strategy. Less is known about the determinants of sec-ond-year-of-life vaccine uptake in sub-Saharan Africa. This study assessed the completion of vac-cines due after 12 months of age, caregiver confidence in newly introduced vaccines, and the factors associated with completion in three districts of Malawi. Methods: A mixed-methods cross-sectional survey of 1,177 caregivers in Chikwawa, Kasungu, and Mangochi assessed household wealth, food insecurity, and vaccination status. The primary outcome was caregiver-reported completion of all vaccines due after 12 months, assessed among children aged 18 months or older. Associations were examined using logistic regression. Focus group discussions with caregivers and key-informant in-terviews with community volunteers and leaders were then analysed thematically to explain the survey findings. Results: Among 1287 age-eligible children,1212 (94.2%) had completed second-year vaccines. Coverage ranged from about 94% for the measles-rubella vaccine to 61% for the Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine. Completion did not differ by household wealth, food insecurity, distance, or caregiver literacy. Health worker reminders were associated with vaccine completion (adjusted odds ratio 0.33, 95% confidence interval 0.17 to 0.62) Infertility concerns about the HPV vaccine were the most commonly reported concern (20.3 %). After excluding age-ineligibility, lack of vaccine awareness was the main reason for children missing newer vaccines. Conclusions: Sec-ond-year coverage was high and equitable. The main modifiable correlate of completion was health worker reminders and follow-up, a function that appears to be under-resourced.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the completion of vaccines due after 12 months of age and the factors associated with completion in three districts of Malawi, addressing the knowledge gap in determinants of second-year vaccine uptake in sub-Saharan Africa.
Design and population
A mixed-methods cross-sectional survey of 1,177 caregivers in Chikwawa, Kasungu, and Mangochi was conducted to assess household wealth, food insecurity, and vaccination status.
Core methods
The study used logistic regression to examine associations and conducted focus group discussions and key-informant interviews to explain the survey findings.
Main results
Among 1,287 age-eligible children, 94.2% had completed second-year vaccines, with coverage ranging from 94% for the measles-rubella vaccine to 61% for the Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine. Health worker reminders were associated with vaccine completion, and infertility concerns about the HPV vaccine were the most commonly reported concern.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found high and equitable second-year coverage, with health worker reminders as a modifiable correlate of completion. The main novel finding was the association between health worker reminders and vaccine completion.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and vaccination strategies, particularly in addressing vaccine hesitancy and ensuring equitable coverage.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and the potential for recall bias. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

马拉维新引入和第二年使用的疫苗供应端瓶颈:从疫苗可获得性到配送

Supply-Side Bottlenecks of Newly Introduced and Second-Year-of-Life Vaccines in Malawi: From Vaccine Availability to Delivery
作者： Beverly Shaila Laher, Asm Shahabuddin, Esther Malata, Susan Barnaba, Hannah Kachule, Lonjezo Dzekedzeke, Beatrice Mkomadzinja, Blessings H Likoswe, Andrew Matchado, Humphrey Chatenga
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
供应端,即系统能否使疫苗可获得、可访问并可配送,却较少受到关注,尽管它决定了愿意接种的照顾者是否能够被服务。
设计与对象
采用混合方法研究,结合照顾者调查、一线卫生工作者调查、设施清单检查和数据质量评估,以及在设施、区级和国家级层面进行的关键人物访谈。
核心方法
在每个步骤中,对疫苗接种率的供应端限制进行了分析,并与每种抗原的覆盖情况进行了对比。
主要结果
疫苗可获得性较高,冷链设备和疫苗在所有检查设施中均有配备,且在50个设施中有47个记录了库存情况,但仍有四分之一的照顾者因缺货而被拒绝接种。
解释与创新
国家级受访者将这些限制归因于一个部分依赖捐助方、且在不同合作伙伴计划之间分散的融资体系。
科研与公卫意义
供应系统能够良好地提供已建立的第二年抗原,但在新抗原和接触环节表现不佳。
局限与证据强度
维持接种率需要保护接种点的库存,为推广活动提供资源,维持一线人员能力,并为新疫苗调整供应体系。

Supply-Side Bottlenecks of Newly Introduced and Second-Year-of-Life Vaccines in Malawi: From Vaccine Availability to Delivery

Authors: Beverly Shaila Laher, Asm Shahabuddin, Esther Malata, Susan Barnaba, Hannah Kachule, Lonjezo Dzekedzeke, Beatrice Mkomadzinja, Blessings H Likoswe, Andrew Matchado, Humphrey Chatenga
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Existing evidence on why children miss vaccines after infancy has focused on the demand side, focusing on the attitudes and circumstances of caregivers. The supply side, meaning the system’s capacity to make vaccines available, accessible, and deliverable, has received less attention, even though it determines whether a willing caregiver can be served. This study examined the supply-side determinants of second-year and newer vaccine uptake in Malawi through a coverage-cascade and bottleneck analysis. Methods: A mixed-methods study drew on a caregiver survey, a survey of frontline health workers, a facility checklist and data quality assessment, and key-informant interviews at facility, district, and national level. Findings were organised through the Tanahashi coverage framework, which follows the steps from the availability of commodities and staff, through geographic access, to service contact and effective coverage. Supply-side constraints in uptake were profiled at each step and set against the coverage achieved for each antigen. Results: Effective coverage fell across the antigen sequence, from 95.9 percent for the second dose of measles-rubella vaccine and 93.1 percent for the second dose of polio vaccine, to 74.6 percent for typhoid, to 63 percent for HPV. Commodity availability was high, with cold chain and vaccines present in every checklist facility, and stock records in 47 of 50, yet a quarter of caregivers had been turned away due to stockouts. Human-resource readiness was weaker, with over half of frontline workers never receiving refresher training. Geographic access constrained a fifth of caregivers, and outreach, though present everywhere, was limited by fuel and, in staff accounts, reduced by the 2025 withdrawal of external funding. The bottleneck was not commodity availability but the contact step and the maintenance of frontline capacity. National respondents placed these constraints within a financing system that is partly donor-dependent and fragmented across separate partner plans. Conclusions: The supply system delivered the established second-year antigens well and faltered for the newer ones and at the point of contact. Sustaining uptake will require protecting stock at the point of need, resourcing outreach, maintaining frontline capacity, and adapting supply for the newer vaccines.

Interpretation and novelty

中国山东省2006–2018年流行性腮腺炎病毒基因型F的全基因组分析

Whole-genome analysis of mumps virus genotype F in Shandong, China (2006–2018)
作者： Suting Wang, Ping Xiong, Yao Liu, Man Zhang, Yiping Feng, Xinyu Yuan, Xia Feng, Zexin Tao, Yingjie Zhang, Xiaolin Jiang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
引言 尽管2008年将含有疫苗的疫苗纳入常规免疫程序,但山东省流行性腮腺炎发病率的发病率仍显著高于麻疹和风疹,且高于世界卫生组织(WHO)消除标准,这提示了流行株的遗传进化可能与此现象有关。
设计与对象
方法对山东省2005年至2024年报告的流行性腮腺炎病例资料进行分析。
核心方法
我们还对同期分离的 16 株腮腺炎病毒株进行了全 基因组 测序。
主要结果
重要的是,在两个时间组之间,其在全基因组水平上与疫苗株S79的同源性(核苷酸或氨基酸)均未发现显著差异。
解释与创新
Conclusion We provides a whole-genome-based, two-time point comparison of genotype F mumps viruses in Shandong across a ~ 12-year interval spanning vaccine introduction (2006–2007 vs.
科研与公卫意义
SH 基因分型一致地将所有 16 个菌株鉴定为基因型 F,这一结果得到了全 基因组 系统发育 的证实;然而,事实证明,单独的 SH 基因不足以解决密切相关菌株之间的系统发育问题,这与 基因组 流行病学 基因组 测序的价值一致。
局限与证据强度
总体而言,2018年山东省的分离株与2006–2007年的分离株相似程度与全国范围内F型病毒株相似,且两组均与疫苗株S79的相似性相当,表明在约12年期间未检测到明显的遗传分化或漂变。

Whole-genome analysis of mumps virus genotype F in Shandong, China (2006–2018)

Authors: Suting Wang, Ping Xiong, Yao Liu, Man Zhang, Yiping Feng, Xinyu Yuan, Xia Feng, Zexin Tao, Yingjie Zhang, Xiaolin Jiang
Original abstract (English)
Introduction Despite the introduction of mumps-containing vaccines into the routine immunization program in 2008, mumps incidence in Shandong Province, China, declined but remained substantially higher than that of measles and rubella, and above WHO elimination thresholds, raising the possibility of whether genetic evolution of circulating strains has contributed to this phenomenon. To address, we compared mumps viruses isolated pre-vaccine (2006–2007) and a decade after rollout (2018). Methods We analyzed mumps case data reported in Shandong from 2005 to 2024. We also performed whole-genome sequencing on 16 mumps virus strains isolated during the same period. Phylogenetic analyses were conducted to characterize viral genetic features and identify mutations. Results From 2005 to 2024, 167,590 mumps cases were reported, peaking in 2012 (25.33 per 100,000 population). SH-gene typing consistently identified all 16 strains as genotype F, a result corroborated by whole-genome phylogeny; however, the SH gene alone proved insufficient for phylogenetic resolution among closely related strains, consistent with the value of whole-genome sequencing for epidemiological surveillance. The 16 full-length genomes shared 98.34–100% nucleotide identity. Phylogenetic analysis revealed intermingling of 2006–2007 and 2018 isolates rather than time-specific clustering, with low inter-group p-distances. Importantly, between the two temporal groups, no significant differences were detected in their respective identities to the vaccine strain S79 at the whole-genome level (nucleotide or amino acid). Two positively selected sites were identified across the seven structural proteins (posterior probability > 0.95). Eight mutations in HN and F that alter hydrophilicity, hydrophobicity, or charge were found, but structural modeling predicted no appreciable conformational changes. Conclusion We provides a whole-genome-based, two-time point comparison of genotype F mumps viruses in Shandong across a ~ 12-year interval spanning vaccine introduction (2006–2007 vs. Overall, the Shandong 2018 isolates remained as similar to the 2006–2007 isolates as F strains are nationwide, and both groups were comparably close to vaccine strain S79, indicating no detectable genetic divergence or drift over the ~12-year period. As the SH gene alone lacked sufficient diversity to resolve closely related isolates, whole-genome sequencing provided more accurate genetic characterization in this study.

Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on abstract only. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

疫苗接种 治疗儿童自身免疫性和自身炎症性风湿病:减毒活疫苗、mRNA 和重组 疫苗 的安全性、免疫原性和有效性证据

Vaccination in Children with Paediatric Autoimmune and Autoinflammatory Rheumatic Diseases: Evidence on Safety, Immunogenicity and Effectiveness Across Live-Attenuated, mRNA and Recombinant Vaccines
作者： Mohammad Hamad Said, Infection & Immunity, Immuno/reuma patientenzorg, Joris van Montfrans, Bas Vastert, Marc Jansen, Joost Swart
摘要中文翻译在患有儿科自身免疫和自身炎症性风湿性疾病(pedAIIRDs)的儿童中,疫苗接种具有临床重要性但具有挑战性。这些儿童由于其基础的免疫介导性疾病和用于疾病控制的免疫抑制治疗,感染风险增加。同时,免疫抑制可能会降低疫苗诱导的免疫应答,而对疾病复发和活疫苗疫苗安全性的担忧可能导致延迟或不完全的疫苗接种。本论文旨在为接受免疫抑制治疗的pedAIIRDs儿童生成具有临床应用价值的现实世界证据,以评估疫苗接种的安全性、免疫原性和有效性。五项研究涵盖了三种疫苗平台:活疫苗麻疹-麻疹-腮腺炎(MMR)加强针疫苗、新冠-19 mRNA疫苗和重组带状疱疹疫苗(RZV;Shingrix)。在一项针对186名幼年特发性关节炎(JIA)儿童的队列研究中,MMR加强针疫苗与疾病活动性增加无显著关联,包括在使用生物制剂疾病修饰抗风湿药物的儿童中,且未观察到严重的疫苗相关不良事件。对182名JIA儿童的长期评估显示,大多数患者在接种疫苗接种后仍保持对麻疹、麻疹和腮腺炎的保护性抗体。然而,接受生物治疗的患者在接种疫苗接种后五年内对麻疹的血清保护率显著低于未接受生物治疗的患者,支持对血清学监测采取针对性策略。一项系统综述和荟萃分析表明,新冠-19疫苗与儿童多系统炎症综合征(MIS-C)风险显著降低相关。在一项前瞻性队列研究中,针对157名儿科风湿病儿童,新冠-19 mRNA疫苗在几乎所有患者中诱导了体液免疫应答,并在大多数检测患者中诱导了足够的T细胞应答。尽管接受生物治疗的患者抗体浓度较低,但疫苗对出现症状的新冠-19感染的保护效果得以保持。未发现疾病活动性增加、心肌炎或严重不良事件。最后,对十名未感染水痘-带状疱疹病毒(varicella-zoster-virus)的免疫功能低下儿童进行了RZV的非标签使用评估,这些儿童因不适合接种活疫苗水痘疫苗而被纳入研究。在接种两剂后,有九名患者出现了血清学转换。其中两名患者在家庭接触后出现了轻度突破性水痘,但未需要住院治疗或抗病毒药物 治疗。综上所述,这些研究支持从广泛避免疫苗接种向免疫功能低下儿童个体化、针对性疫苗接种策略的转变。研究结果为选择性使用活疫苗、长期针对性监测疫苗诱导的免疫应答、有效使用mRNA疫苗以及进一步评估非活疫苗替代方案提供了证据。这些结果可能有助于未来国家和国际层面针对免疫介导炎症疾病的儿童制定疫苗接种建议。

在一项前瞻性队列研究中,针对157名儿科风湿病儿童,新冠-19 mRNA疫苗在几乎所有患者中诱导了体液免疫应答,并在大多数检测患者中诱导了足够的T细胞应答。
